windows close\n\n$matrixDir = \"$env:USERPROFILE\\Documents\\Matrix\"\n\n# Import layout engine\n. \"$PSScriptRoot\\WindowLayoutEngine.ps1\"\n\n# Add Window API\nAdd-Type -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue -TypeDefinition @\"\nusing System;\nusing System.Collections.Generic;\nusing System.Runtime.InteropServices;\nusing System.Text;\nusing System.Diagnostics;\n\npublic class MonitorAPI {\n    public delegate bool EnumWindowsProc(IntPtr hWnd, IntPtr lParam);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    public static extern bool EnumWindows(EnumWindowsProc lpEnumFunc, IntPtr lParam);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\", CharSet = CharSet.Auto)]\n    public static extern int GetWindowText(IntPtr hWnd, StringBuilder lpString, int nMaxCount);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    public static extern bool IsWindowVisible(IntPtr hWnd);\n\n    [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")]\n    public static extern uint GetWindowThreadProcessId(IntPtr hWnd, out uint lpdwProcessId);\n\n    private static List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>> foundWindows;\n\n    public static List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>> FindMatrixWindows() {\n        foundWindows = new List<KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>>();\n        EnumWindows((hWnd, lParam) => {\n            if (IsWindowVisible(hWnd)) {\n                uint processId;\n                GetWindowThreadProcessId(hWnd, out processId);\n                try {\n                    var process = Process.GetProcessById((int)processId);\n                    if (process.ProcessName.Equals(\"WindowsTerminal\", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) {\n                        var sb = new StringBuilder(256);\n                        GetWindowText(hWnd, sb, 256);\n                        var title = sb.ToString();\n                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(title) && title.Contains(\"Matrix-\") && !title.Contains(\"Redpill\")) {\n                            foundWindows.Add(new KeyValuePair<IntPtr, string>(hWnd, title));\n                        }\n                    }\n                } catch { }\n            }\n            return true;\n        }, IntPtr.Zero);\n        return foundWindows;\n    }\n}\n\"@\n\nfunction Get-MatrixWindowHandles {\n    $windows = [MonitorAPI]::FindMatrixWindows()\n    $result = @()\n    foreach ($w in $windows) {\n        if ($w.Value -match \"Matrix-(\\d+)\") {\n            $result += @{\n                Handle = $w.Key\n                Slot = [int]$Matches[1]\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return $result\n}\n\nfunction Position-AllMatrixWindows {\n    $windowInfo = Get-MatrixWindowHandles\n    if ($windowInfo.Count -eq 0) { return }\n\n    $windowHandles = @{}\n    foreach ($win in $windowInfo) {\n        $windowHandles[\"Matrix-$($win.Slot)\"] = @{ Handle = $win.Handle }\n    }\n\n    try {\n        $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n        $mode = if ($config.Mode) { $config.Mode } else { 'Pillars' }\n        Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout -WindowHandles $windowHandles -Mode $mode | Out-Null\n    }\n    catch { }\n}\n\n# Settings\n$pollIntervalMs = 200   # Check every 200ms\n$dragSettleMs = 400     # Wait after drag detected before repositioning\n$noWindowsTimeout = 5000  # Exit after 5 seconds with no Matrix windows\n\nWrite-Host \"Matrix Window Monitor started\" -ForegroundColor Cyan\nWrite-Host \"Monitoring for window drags... (Ctrl+C to stop)\" -ForegroundColor DarkGray\n\n$noWindowsTime = 0\n$initialized = $false\n\nwhile ($true) {\n    $windowInfo = Get-MatrixWindowHandles\n    $handles = @($windowInfo | ForEach-Object { $_.Handle })\n\n    # Exit if no Matrix windows for too long\n    if ($handles.Count -eq 0) {\n        $noWindowsTime += $pollIntervalMs\n        if ($noWindowsTime -ge $noWindowsTimeout) {\n            Write-Host \"No Matrix windows detected for $($noWindowsTimeout/1000)s. Exiting.\" -ForegroundColor Yellow\n            exit 0\n        }\n        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $pollIntervalMs\n        continue\n    }\n    $noWindowsTime = 0\n\n    # Initialize tracking if needed\n    if (-not $initialized -or $handles.Count -ne $script:LastKnownPositions.Count) {\n        Initialize-PositionTracking -WindowHandles $handles\n        $initialized = $true\n    }\n\n    # Check for drag\n    if (Test-WindowDragDetected -WindowHandles $handles) {\n        Write-Host \"Drag detected - repositioning...\" -ForegroundColor Green\n        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $dragSettleMs\n        Position-AllMatrixWindows\n        Update-PositionTracking -WindowHandles $handles\n    }\n\n    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $pollIntervalMs\n}\n","numLines":134,"startLine":1,"totalLines":134}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3f26a941-7187-4570-9a59-001d3979cb8d"}

Now I have all the information needed. Let me create a comprehensive analysis report:\n\n## MATRIX TERMINAL - POSITION TRACKING & DRAG DETECTION EXPLORATION REPORT\n\n### EXECUTIVE SUMMARY\n\nThe Matrix Terminal project implements a background drag-snap monitor system that automatically repositions windows when users drag them. The system was added in the most recent commit (863ffbe) and consists of three main components:\n\n1. **WindowLayoutEngine.ps1** - Position tracking and layout calculation\n2. **matrix_monitor.ps1** - Background process that polls for drags\n3. **Bluepill/Redpill/matrix_control.ps1** - Launch and management scripts\n\n---\n\n### 1. WINDOW POSITION TRACKING\n\n#### Location: `WindowLayoutEngine.ps1` (Lines 1193-1346)\n\n**Current Tracking Approach:**\n\n```powershell\n# Module-level state (lines 1195-1197)\n$script:LastKnownPositions = @{}\n$script:DragThreshold = 50  # Minimum pixels of movement to consider a drag\n```\n\n**Key Functions:**\n\n1. **`Get-WindowPositions` (Lines 1216-1244)**\n   - Uses Windows API `GetWindowRect()` to retrieve current window dimensions\n   - Returns hashtable: `@{ \"handle_string\" = @{ X, Y, Width, Height } }`\n   - Called every polling interval to capture current state\n   - Silently skips invalid/closed windows\n\n2. **`Initialize-PositionTracking` (Lines 1260-1268)**\n   - Called once at startup or when window count changes\n   - Captures baseline positions for all tracked windows\n   - Stores in `$script:LastKnownPositions` global variable\n   - Logs: \"Initialized position tracking for N windows\"\n\n3. **`Update-PositionTracking` (Lines 1338-1346)**\n   - Called after successful repositioning\n   - Refreshes the baseline positions\n   - Prevents \"flickering\" drag detections (prevents re-triggering immediately after reposition)\n\n---\n\n### 2. DRAG DETECTION LOGIC\n\n#### Location: `WindowLayoutEngine.ps1` (Lines 1289-1323)\n\n**Function: `Test-WindowDragDetected`**\n\n```powershell\nfunction Test-WindowDragDetected {\n    param([array]$WindowHandles)\n    \n    # If no baseline, initialize and return false (first call)\n    if ($script:LastKnownPositions.Count -eq 0) {\n        Initialize-PositionTracking -WindowHandles $WindowHandles\n        return $false\n    }\n    \n    # Get current positions\n    $currentPositions = Get-WindowPositions -WindowHandles $WindowHandles\n    \n    # Check each window for movement\n    foreach ($handleKey in $currentPositions.Keys) {\n        # New window appeared = drag event\n        if (-not $script:LastKnownPositions.ContainsKey($handleKey)) {\n            return $true\n        }\n        \n        $current = $currentPositions[$handleKey]\n        $last = $script:LastKnownPositions[$handleKey]\n        \n        # Calculate movement in pixels\n        $deltaX = [Math]::Abs($current.X - $last.X)\n        $deltaY = [Math]::Abs($current.Y - $last.Y)\n        \n        # Threshold check: 50 pixels minimum movement\n        if ($deltaX -gt $script:DragThreshold -or $deltaY -gt $script:DragThreshold) {\n            return $true\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return $false\n}\n```\n\n**Drag Detection Criteria:**\n\n1. **Pixel Movement Threshold**: 50 pixels (configurable via `$script:DragThreshold`)\n2. **Detection Triggers**:\n   - X position changed by >50 pixels\n   - Y position changed by >50 pixels\n   - New window appeared (window count mismatch)\n3. **What Doesn't Count**:\n   - Resize operations (Width/Height changes are ignored)\n   - Sub-threshold movements (<50 pixels)\n   - Window becoming visible/invisible\n\n---\n\n### 3. MATRIX_MONITOR.PS1 - THE BACKGROUND PROCESS\n\n#### Location: `matrix_monitor.ps1` (Complete file)\n\n**Purpose**: Continuous background polling daemon that monitors windows and snaps them back.\n\n**Architecture:**\n\n```\nMain Loop (202ms interval)\n├─ Get-MatrixWindowHandles (find all \"Matrix-N\" windows except Redpill)\n├─ Check for timeout (5s with no windows = exit)\n├─ Initialize-PositionTracking (on first run or window count change)\n├─ Test-WindowDragDetected (compare current vs. baseline positions)\n│  └─ If detected:\n│     ├─ Log \"Drag detected - repositioning...\"\n│     ├─ Wait 400ms for drag to settle\n│     ├─ Position-AllMatrixWindows (invoke layout engine)\n│     └─ Update-PositionTracking (refresh baseline)\n└─ Sleep 200ms\n```\n\n**Key Settings (Lines 91-94):**\n\n| Setting | Value | Purpose |\n|---------|-------|---------|\n| `$pollIntervalMs` | 200 | Check window positions every 200ms |\n| `$dragSettleMs` | 400 | Wait 400ms after drag before repositioning |\n| `$noWindowsTimeout` | 5000 | Exit after 5 seconds with no Matrix windows |\n\n**Window Detection (Lines 60-72):**\n- Searches for ALL visible Windows Terminal windows\n- Filters: Title must contain \"Matrix-\" but NOT \"Redpill\"\n- Uses P/Invoke `EnumWindows` for window enumeration\n- Extracts slot number from title: `Matrix-1`, `Matrix-2`, etc.\n\n---\n\n### 4. WHAT HAPPENS WHEN A WINDOW IS DRAGGED\n\n**The Complete \"Deport Back\" Flow:**\n\n```\nUser drags Matrix-1 window manually\n    ↓\nOS moves window (SetWindowPos called by user drag)\n    ↓\nmatrix_monitor.ps1 loop (every 200ms):\n  - Reads new position via GetWindowRect()\n  - Compares to LastKnownPositions\n  - Detects deltaX or deltaY > 50 pixels\n    ↓\n  Drag detected! Log message:\n  \"Drag detected - repositioning...\" (Green)\n    ↓\n  Wait 400ms (dragSettleMs)\n    → Allows user to finish dragging\n    → Prevents multiple repositioning events\n    ↓\n  Position-AllMatrixWindows called:\n    1. Get-MatrixWindowHandles (find all Matrix windows)\n    2. Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout (layout engine)\n       - Reads layout mode from matrix_state.json (Pillars/Quads)\n       - Calculates target positions using Get-PillarsLayout or Get-QuadsLayout\n       - Calls SetWindowPos for EACH window\n    3. Update-PositionTracking (refresh baseline)\n       - New positions stored in $script:LastKnownPositions\n       - Prevents re-triggering drag detection\n    ↓\n  Loop continues - window is now at calculated layout position\n```\n\n**Why \"Deport Back\" Occurs:**\n\nThe window is forced back to its calculated slot position because:\n\n1. **Aggressive Repositioning**: Every drag larger than 50 pixels triggers immediate repositioning\n2. **No User Preference Storage**: System only respects the layout algorithm output, not manual positioning\n3. **Layout Configuration Priority**: Once Pillars or Quads mode is set in matrix_state.json, ALL windows snap to those coordinates\n4. **No \"Pin\" or \"Lock\" Mode**: User has no way to opt-out of repositioning\n\n---\n\n### 5. POSITION PERSISTENCE\n\n#### Location: `WindowLayoutEngine.ps1` (Lines 1350-1519)\n\n**Saving Positions: `Save-WindowPositions` (Lines 1365-1429)**\n\n```powershell\n# Stores to matrix_state.json under windowPositions key\n$state.windowPositions = @{\n    \"Matrix-1\" = @{ x = 60; y = 90; width = 560; height = 930 }\n    \"Matrix-2\" = @{ x = 680; y = 90; width = 560; height = 930 }\n    ...\n}\n$state.positionsSavedAt = ISO8601_timestamp\n```\n\n- **When Called**: After repositioning via layout engine\n- **Format**: Keyed by shader name (Matrix-1, Matrix-2, etc.)\n- **Atomic Write**: Uses temp file + Move-Item pattern to prevent corruption (US-001)\n\n**Restoring Positions: `Restore-WindowPositions` (Lines 1454-1519)**\n\n```powershell\n# Called by bluepill.ps1 on startup\nif (Restore-WindowPositions -WindowInfo $windowInfo) {\n    # Success - positions restored from last session\n    return\n} else {\n    # No saved positions - fall back to layout engine\n    Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout -WindowHandles $windowHandles -Mode 'Auto'\n}\n```\n\n- **When Called**: Blueprint on startup (Lines 204-208 of bluepill.ps1)\n- **Behavior**: Chrome-like window restoration from previous session\n- **Fallback**: If no saved positions, uses layout engine\n\n---\n\n### 6. CURRENT STATE STRUCTURE\n\n#### Location: `matrix_state.json`\n\n```json\n{\n  \"lastSlots\": [1, 2, 3, 4, 5],\n  \"positionsSavedAt\": \"2026-01-19T13:21:28...\",\n  \"lastSaved\": \"2026-01-19T13:21:24...\",\n  \"windowPositions\": {\n    \"Matrix-1\": { \"x\": 60, \"y\": 90, \"width\": 560, \"height\": 930 },\n    \"Matrix-2\": { \"x\": 680, \"y\": 90, \"width\": 560, \"height\": 930 },\n    \"Matrix-3\": { \"x\": 1070, \"y\": 331, \"width\": 560, \"height\": 930 },\n    \"Matrix-4\": { \"x\": 867, \"y\": 206, \"width\": 870, \"height\": 930 },\n    \"Matrix-5\": { \"x\": 2910, \"y\": 463, \"width\": 870, \"height\": 930 }\n  },\n  \"layout\": {\n    \"mode\": \"Pillars\",\n    \"maxPillarsPerScreen\": 4,\n    \"gapSize\": 60,\n    \"preferredScreen\": 0,\n    \"windowsOnPrimary\": null\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### 7. ROOT CAUSE OF \"DEPORT BACK\" BEHAVIOR\n\n**Problem**: Dragging a window causes it to immediately snap back to its layout-calculated position.\n\n**Root Causes:**\n\n1. **No Drag Prevention**: Layout engine runs continuously regardless of user intent\n2. **Threshold Too Sensitive**: 50-pixel threshold catches minor drags\n3. **No State Preservation**: Dragged positions are not saved; only layout-calculated positions matter\n4. **Aggressive Monitor Loop**: 200ms polling means positions are checked constantly\n5. **No User Override**: System has no way to know if user wants to keep manual position or revert to layout\n\n**Example**:\n- User drags Matrix-1 from (60, 90) to (200, 150)\n- Monitor detects: deltaX=140, deltaY=60 (both > 50px threshold)\n- Drag detected! → 400ms settle → Layout Engine repositions back to (60, 90)\n- User sees window \"bounce back\"\n\n---\n\n### 8. INTEGRATION POINTS\n\n**Where Monitor is Launched:**\n\n1. **bluepill.ps1** (Lines 266-272):\n   ```powershell\n   Start-Process powershell -ArgumentList \"...matrix_monitor.ps1\" -WindowStyle Hidden\n   Write-Host \" Window monitor started (drag-snap enabled)\"\n   ```\n\n2. **matrix_setup.ps1**: Similar launch pattern\n\n3. **matrix_control.ps1**: Similar launch pattern\n\n**Monitor Lifecycle:**\n- Starts: When bluepill is run\n- Runs: Independent background process (hidden PowerShell window)\n- Exits: After 5 seconds with no Matrix windows detected\n\n---\n\n### 9. KEY TECHNICAL DETAILS\n\n**Windows API Calls Used:**\n\n| API | Purpose |\n|-----|---------|\n| `EnumWindows` | Find all visible windows |\n| `GetWindowText` | Read window title (for \"Matrix-N\" detection) |\n| `GetWindowRect` | Get current X, Y, Width, Height |\n| `SetWindowPos` | Move/resize window |\n| `GetWindowThreadProcessId` | Filter to Windows Terminal only |\n| `IsWindowVisible` | Ensure window is visible |\n\n**Performance Characteristics:**\n\n- **Memory**: Stores positions for 6 windows max = ~200 bytes\n- **CPU**: 200ms polling = 5 checks/second (minimal)\n- **Latency**: 200ms poll + 400ms settle = 600ms before repositioning starts\n- **Jitter**: No; positions are absolute, not relative\n\n---\n\n### 10. SUMMARY TABLE\n\n| Aspect | Details |\n|--------|---------|\n| **Tracking Method** | Periodic polling (200ms) + GetWindowRect API |\n| **Baseline Storage** | `$script:LastKnownPositions` hashtable (in-process) |\n| **Drag Detection** | >50px movement in X or Y axis |\n| **Drag Response** | 400ms settle → reposition → update baseline |\n| **Repositioning** | Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout (Pillars/Quads layout) |\n| **Position Persistence** | matrix_state.json (atomic writes) |\n| **Monitor Process** | Hidden PowerShell background process |\n| **Monitor Exit Condition** | 5 seconds without Matrix windows |\n| **Why \"Deport Back\"** | Aggressive re-positioning overrides user's manual move |\n\n---\n\n### CONCLUSION\n\nThe current position tracking system is straightforward but aggressive:\n\n1. **Monitor continuously polls** every 200ms checking for position changes\n2. **Detects drags** when movement exceeds 50 pixels\n3. **Waits 400ms** for drag to settle\n4. **Repositions all windows** to layout-calculated positions\n5. **Updates baseline** to prevent re-triggering\n\nThe \"deport back\" behavior is a design consequence of prioritizing the layout algorithm over user preferences. Users cannot override or customize window positions permanently because the system treats all positions as temporary and recalculates them on every layout cycle."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2096,"cache_read_input_tokens":61169,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2096,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CXHJjvYTZzRtgdbJBVq9N","type":"assistant","uuid":"9ab0e346-cba1-4f9d-9574-f1402c94a4a8","timestamp":"2026-01-19T19:42:23.264Z"}
